NOW THEREFORE, for and consideration of the foregoing and by virtue of the provision of
Presidential Decree NO. 1216, which provides that upon completion of the development of the
subdivision as certified by the Housing Land Use and Regulatory Board, the roads, alleys, and open
spaces which includes community facility and parks and playground shall be donated to the Local
Government and it shall be mandatory for the Local Government to accept the same, the DONOR by
these presents do hereby CEDES, TRANSFERS, DISPOSE and CONVEYS by way of DONATION unto
the said DONEE the aforementioned parcels of land to the DONEE;

Finally, the DONEE do hereby accept the donation of the aforementioned parcels of land and
further express its most sincere appreciation and gratitude and for the kindness and liberality
shown by the DONOR.
